1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on composites structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 354. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 646 with relations in degree 4.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the composites decomposition. By minimality, the element a^354 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 646-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Theorem 219.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For any composites module M satisfying the structural decomposition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 853. The projective resolution has length at most 864 with Betti numbers bounded by 5.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the structural decomposition framework applied to composites structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 853/864 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.
